([AndreiuMarchenko/refactor-TypeScript-React-skill](https://github.com/AndreiuMarchenko/refactor-TypeScript-React-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★6)
- **source-driven-skill**: Grounds every implementation decision in official documentation or local source code. Use when building with any framework, library, or SDK where correctness matters. Use when an agent is likely to hallucinate API names, or when docs are weak and the library source is available locally.
